# HebrewEdu knowledge inventory — 2026-10-12

**Phase 1: investigation and cleanup proposal only.** No existing files were deleted, moved, renamed or rewritten; no website changes, API requests, Rank Tracker execution, builds, commits or pushes were made. Only this inventory and `knowledge-structure-proposal.md` were added under website `docs/`. The final project knowledge index has not been created.

## 1. Coverage and method

Recursively enumerated both exact roots, including hidden files, `.local/`, `artifacts/`, research caches, scripts, tests, deployment configuration and product assets:

- `/Users/kasutaja/Developer/seo-research/hebrewedu/`
- `/Users/kasutaja/Developer/web/hebrewedu-site/`

Excluded directory trees named `node_modules`, `dist` and `.git` from the detailed inventory. `.astro/` is included because two support files are tracked and it exposes a specific cleanup distinction. Existing website files total **865** and research files **139**, for **1,004** pre-existing files. These two newly generated deliverables are intentionally excluded from their own snapshot counts.

For every file, recorded absolute path, extension/format, byte count, filesystem last-modified time in **UTC**, SHA-256, Git status, content-derived purpose/type, incoming references and proposed disposition. Modification time is not a research observation/fetch date. Text/schema/header/code structures were inspected; binary signatures/dimensions were checked where available. Screenshots/audio were not individually evaluated for visual or linguistic quality. No lesson prose, answer payloads, capability URLs, credentials or private snapshot contents are reproduced in the inventory.

Dependency discovery covered Markdown links, literal paths/filenames, public asset URLs, absolute evidence paths in reports/datasets, code imports and known dynamic directory consumers. Missing links were resolved relative to the document's actual location. Public blog/game/course routes were distinguished from filesystem links. Git tracking/ignore status came from Git; file-path presence was checked across all available local Git history, and textual mentions were searched in historical documentation/script/test patches. No private script was executed.

This is a static reference inventory, not a complete language-aware dependency proof: dynamically constructed paths, runtime external consumers and unqualified filenames may need manual review. “No literal incoming reference found” does not establish safe deletion. Git-history mentions are evidence of historical coupling, not a current dependency. Research is **outside any detected Git repository**; absence of Git tracking is not deletion authorization or evidence that originals are backed up elsewhere.

**Authority follows category, not newest modification time.** Current publication is governed by the website release manifest and code. Original source bytes remain authoritative for their dated acquisition. Semantic Core v2 is the current research edition, while its no-publication statements describe the research run's scope. They must not override the later approved release.

### Measurement records that must remain separate

- Original GSC workbooks referenced by research are in `/private/tmp/hebrewedu-seo-audit-2026-10-10/`, outside both roots. They contain aggregate Web/all-country/all-device evidence. Saved homepage Pages observations are 31 clicks/862 impressions/3.60% CTR/9.72 position for the three-month period and 15/346/4.34%/8.47 for its latest 28-day period. Preserve exact workbook filters/windows and disclosure limits.
- The later **owner-supplied exact-homepage baseline** is Web, last 28 days, **17 clicks / 356 impressions / 4.8% CTR / 8.8 position**. This was supplied before title implementation in this conversation. No corresponding export file or exact calendar dates were located in the two roots; do not invent them or overwrite the older workbook values. It is observational, not a promised improvement. A later index/status record should cite this as owner-supplied until the exact filtered export is preserved.
- Rank Tracker has **one** complete October 10 depth-20 run: `337cdd21-2385-43df-aafb-5b086da27385`, 36 planned/request/observation rows. Its SQLite also has one separate historic depth-10 pilot. The complete run observed homepage rank 7 for `hebrew alphabet app`. `pre-baseline` exports have blanks and are not duplicate measurements.
- V1 pilot and v2 alphabet SERPs use dated depth-10 observations; the Rank Tracker depth-20 baseline is a different observation set. Provider-null, not requested, transport/provider error, numeric zero and absent returned organic row retain distinct meanings.

## 3. Duplication, supersession and conflicts

### Exact duplicates

SHA-256 found **147 groups containing 433 files**. The arithmetic duplicate-byte total is **41,914,410 bytes**, not an estimate of safely recoverable space. Each complete group and SHA-256 is listed later. No duplicate was deleted.

| Duplicate family | Groups | Decision |
|---|---:|---|
| Release preservation copies / HTTP capture overlapping working source and earlier QA | 111 | ARCHIVE as dated evidence bundles; do not casually remove copies or rewrite captured relative links |
| Identical audio distributed with the two self-contained games | 22 | KEEP both distributions; synced runtime contracts need local assets, and game source authority is a separate project |
| Repeated QA screenshots outside the release-preservation category | 8 | ARCHIVE as dated runs; establish retention policy before any deletion |
| Other validation/assets, including log/JSON correspondence and screenshot source copies | 5 | KEEP or ARCHIVE by role; identical bytes alone do not decide ownership |
| Research owner-letter map and four website/import preservation copies | 1 | KEEP all checksum-bound copies; planning map is not publication authority |

Important exceptions:

- `course-letter-map-v2.json`, `scripts/alphabet-course/owner-letter-map.v2.json`, two private import manifests and the release before-copy share SHA-256 **20521e78580146ee94bfafc4b391bb8bfcd5832943300852c8b446d4f547e3c8**. Import/recovery scripts and tests depend on the importer-side copy and its hash. Do not replace it with a symlink or delete run-local manifests just to reduce duplication.
- `src/assets/fonts/Rubik-OFL.txt`, `public/licenses/Rubik-OFL.txt` and its saved live HTTP body match. Source and distributed license copies serve different obligations; KEEP both.
- `public/screenshots/app-home.jpg` and `src/assets/screenshots/home.jpg` match. The public copy is used by application schema; the source copy feeds the optimized image pipeline. KEEP both while these consumers exist.
- Original Aleph source appears in its snapshot location, import run and immutable correction-package copy. These copies preserve acquisition/correction provenance and are protected even where exact hashes match.

### Near-duplicates and overlapping reports

Markdown token-set comparison (Jaccard ≥0.42, exact duplicates excluded, frozen before-copies excluded) found the complete and pre-baseline Rank Tracker reports similar at **0.745**. Their shared template does not make observations interchangeable. Manual content review additionally identified these overlapping responsibilities:

| Files / cluster | What overlaps | Explicit resolution |
|---|---|---|
| `keyword-candidates.csv`, qualified/volume editions, Semantic Core v1/v2 | Same foundation candidates with qualification/measurement fields added | KEEP all stage datasets. V2 is current edition; v1 and stage exports are historical/provenance inputs, not disposable duplicates. Original v1 fields verified unchanged in v2. |
| `serp-pilot-analysis.md`, `serp-interpretation.md`, `keyword-to-url-map.md` | Pilot observations → interpretation → editorial assignment | KEEP complementary evidence layers. Date/settings/result rows support interpretation; mapping does not become a measured query × URL fact. |
| `demand-analysis.md`, `keyword-difficulty-analysis.md`, `content-audit-v1.md`, `seo-roadmap-90-days.md` | Demand, provider KD, content decisions and task planning | KEEP evidence reports; CONSOLIDATE roadmap status into a future dated implementation ledger without rewriting research observations. |
| Course investigation, v2 research report and implementation roadmap | Original proposal and measured expansion plus staged future implementation | ARCHIVE original investigation; KEEP v2 research; CONSOLIDATE completed milestones and remaining gates into implementation workflow, preserving original plan. |
| Hub/POC docs and `hebrew-alphabet-release.md` | Architecture, UX, preview gates and released behavior | CONSOLIDATE reusable architecture/UX details. Release document/code control present availability. Preserve historical POC validation. |
| Editorial review → source-edit checklist → blocked sync → owner override → readiness → release | Changing owner/content decisions for the same Aleph IDs | ARCHIVE each earlier gate with explicit successor links. Final accepted source, empty hold sets and release supersede 11-question/Q4-False/publication-disabled requirements. Do not flatten these decisions into one undated claim. |
| Main `docs/` and `.local/alphabet-release/.../working-tree-before/` | Byte-identical preservation copies of docs/scripts/source | Current working file controls current implementation; frozen copies prove pre-release state. ARCHIVE whole evidence bundle without rewriting it. |

### Concrete conflicts and stale states

1. **POC checker conflict:** `scripts/check-lesson-1-artifacts.mjs` asserts Aleph absent from the normal build and no `/learn/` sitemap entries, and tests a noindex preview. `scripts/check-alphabet-release.mjs` and `docs/hebrew-alphabet-release.md` describe the current published state. The former is an ARCHIVE candidate, not a current release test. Importer README and historical docs still reference it; references must be revised in a separately approved phase before moving it. Do not run it and treat the expected conflict as a new website regression.
2. **Editorial acceptance conflict:** source-edit checklist/blocked-sync requirements include Q4 False and additional wording gates. Owner override accepts Q4 True; final readiness/release accept unchanged B2/B6 and all 14 exercises. Old private `verify-package.mjs` validates its historical package, not today's release. Preserve rather than mutate it.
3. **Research planning versus deployment:** original investigation proposes 171 additions / 307 combined rows; measured v2 has 173 additions / 309 combined rows. The cache says later lessons remain uninspected; complete import has 22 sources. Their findings are dated scopes, not current deployment facts. The v2 metrics remain current research; stale implementation status must be indexed as historical.
4. **Homepage audit/title:** audit metadata table retains the old title and proposes an app-title test with a brand suffix. Owner instead approved the exact no-suffix title deployed by `579ef938`. Keep the audit as its dated observation; future current-state index must mark that recommendation completed with the actual string.
5. **README architecture:** component tree names old `src/components/Hero.astro`, `Features.astro`, `HowItWorks.astro` etc.; current home components are under `src/components/home/`. Screenshot instructions name `.jpg` assets while retained legacy files are `.png` and current image pipeline uses `src/assets/screenshots/`. CONSOLIDATE overview with reviewed current architecture later.
6. **Keyword map paths:** prose uses `/blog/why-hebrew-handwriting-looks-completely-different-from-printed-hebrew.md`, `/blog/how-the-hebrew-root-system-works.md`, `/blog/hebrew-binyanim-explained.md`. These mix source filenames with public URLs. Current public pages use extensionless trailing-slash routes. Preserve assignment/history and later correct notation in an explicitly reviewed current navigation/ownership summary; do not silently alter v1/v2 fields.
7. **Research cache:** `.seo-cache/dataforseo.json` contains valid v2 measurement summary but stale uninspected-lessons/research-only recommendations. REVIEW as a derivative; it cannot outrank original responses, completed import or current release manifest.

## 4. References, protected dependencies and unindexed knowledge

### Important dependency chains

| Chain | Dependency / consequence |
|---|---|
| Semantic Core v1 → v2 and method/demand/SERP/KD | Preserve exact original fields, provider statuses and dates. Reports are interpretations; raw provider envelopes remain primary acquisition evidence. |
| `rank-tracking-keywords.csv` → `approved-keywords.sha256` → tracker | Input checksum binds the approved 36-query queue. A URL/keyword change is a reviewed future configuration change, not documentation cleanup. |
| tracker → `schema.sql`, `history.sqlite3`, response hashes, pricing/pilot evidence, reports | SQLite history is append-only by triggers. Preserve database, original responses and run metadata together; report export is derived. `history.lock` is operational coordination, not an obsolete report. Do not run tracker/init/report for this phase. |
| research `course-letter-map-v2.json` → importer-side map → run-local manifest hashes | Stable ID/order/slug source is shared as pinned copies. `recover.mjs`, importer and tests use exact hashes; cleanup cannot deduplicate these by changing paths/content. |
| `.local/alphabet-course-imports/v1/<run>/import-state.json` → `audit-result.json` → versioned audit/validation/review snapshot | Latest pointer selects `audit-v1-595de65ea735`; older audits preserve validator evolution. `course-content.snapshot.v1.json` is deliberately absent while six sources retain 11 structural empty records. This is not a missing file to recreate. |
| original and two timestamped Aleph snapshots → normalizer/tests/correction verifier | Normalizer pins final 19:30 source checksum; tests conditionally use original/intermediate/private sources. These three ages cannot be collapsed into a generic current `lesson-1.raw.json`. |
| normalized Aleph model + presentation → lesson UI/grading/tests | `src/content/lessons/aleph.json` controls approved learner data; empty hold sets mean all 14 active. Private raw imports are not build inputs. |
| `src/data/alphabet-course.json` → route/library/nav/schema/sitemap/tests | Only Hub and Aleph published; 21 non-interactive Coming Soon cards. Planning map and imported availability cannot bypass the publication manifest. |
| `docs/hebrew-alphabet-release.md` → current release checker/browser/hub scripts | Current validation entry point. Historical POC checker uses different assumptions and old baselines. |
| `.local/course-hub/production-before.json` and `.local/lesson-1-production-baseline.json` → historic/current optional baseline consumers | Never regenerate over historic baseline to hide changes. Later checks should choose an explicit appropriate baseline. |
| game runbook → sync scripts → `public/game-builds/<game>/` | Public game folders are intentional deployable distributions, not research files. Duplication across distributions belongs to game-source architecture, outside this cleanup. |
| Rubik source license → public distributed license | Two required roles; KEEP exact license copies. |

### Link findings

**74 missing relative Markdown target occurrences** were found, all inside frozen `.local/alphabet-release/20261011T194531Z/working-tree-before/docs/` copies: 50 in the POC copy, 21 in the hub copy and 3 in the course-content-audit copy. They refer to original-root `artifacts/` or `.local/` evidence, but resolve relative to the nested frozen copy. Their corresponding original docs targets exist. Do not “repair” frozen evidence; index its original root/base so readers resolve the preserved document in context.

No missing filesystem Markdown target was found in the active top-level `docs/` or research Markdown after distinguishing public URLs. This is not a claim that every fragment, external web link, prose source path or dynamic expression is correct. The three `.md` public-route notations above are independently verified ambiguous notation. Loopback preview URLs and temporary build directories are historical links, not durable entry points.

There are **64 ambiguous unqualified filename occurrences**, often because the same name exists in current source and frozen before-copies, or many runs have `validation.json`. All occurrences and candidate file IDs are listed below. Future current docs should use root-relative full paths plus run ID rather than `validation.json`, `index.astro` or a screenshot basename alone.

### No incoming literal document references found

These useful files lack incoming literal references from the pre-existing two-root inventory and need the future knowledge index, not deletion:

- `candidate-methodology.md`: foundational source/qualification method and external GSC provenance manifest.
- `keyword-difficulty-analysis.md`: exact organic metric interpretation and limits.
- `rank-tracker/README.md`: executable history/settings/approval protocol entry point.
- `hebrew-alphabet-course-investigation.md`: historical original proposal; index in archive history.
- `docs/seo-homepage-semantic-core-v2-audit.md`: completed October 12 audit, currently untracked and not linked from README.

Neither root has a project-wide current knowledge index. Research has 139 files but no root README/start-here guide. Important course/import docs/scripts/tests are untracked locally; research has no detected Git versioning. `.local/` and POC artifacts are ignored deliberately, not guaranteed backed up. Do not treat untracked/ignored status as expendability. A future ownership/backup plan should preserve private evidence separately from public deployment and review untracked source scripts/editorial excerpts before any Git inclusion.

## 6. Cleanup recommendation and counts

Disposition is a **proposal**, not authorization. ARCHIVE means retain the evidence and label/index it as historical; it does not require immediate relocation. CONSOLIDATE means a reviewed future extract/merge of reusable/current instructions while preserving dated originals. DELETE CANDIDATE means demonstrably regenerable OS/interpreter/Astro output, still subject to owner approval and a final dependency check. REVIEW means evidence is insufficient to remove or declare current authority.

| Disposition | Files | Proposed action |
|---|---:|---|
| KEEP | 399 | Preserve authoritative/current source, original data and useful dated reports. |
| ARCHIVE | 574 | Label/index historical decisions and QA bundles; preserve bytes and dependency paths. |
| CONSOLIDATE | 5 | Review five overlapping overview/workflow/roadmap files; keep originals until successor accepted. |
| DELETE CANDIDATE | 15 | Only generated OS/interpreter/ignored Astro support artifacts; no research or snapshot originals. |
| REVIEW | 11 | Resolve cache/stale source README, importer instructions, tracked generated support and potentially unused assets/components. |

**Suggested first approval scope:** establish the index/status chronology, preserve GSC originals in durable private research storage, consolidate current course/README instructions, and label the historical POC checker. Do not approve blanket duplicate deletion. Raw snapshots, provider envelopes, historic baselines, database/run records, legal license copies and self-contained game assets are protected by default.

No dependency-free deletion of historical reports is recommended. Fifteen deletion candidates are three `.DS_Store` files, one Python `.pyc`, and eleven ignored generated `.astro/` files. Two tracked `.astro/` support files are REVIEW, not deletion candidates. Five legacy public screenshots and the apparently unused ProductPlaceholder component are REVIEW because historical/external consumers cannot be ruled out.

## 11. Owner review boundary

Approve a specific Phase 2 scope before any migration, rewrite, archival move or deletion. The companion [knowledge structure proposal](knowledge-structure-proposal.md) defines a minimal index-first approach, explicit retention exceptions, dependencies and rollback checks. This inventory freezes what exists today; it does not grant publication or lesson approval, authorize paid tracking, or replace research with an undated combined summary.
